Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the symmetric framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 136. The resulting operator achieves bound O(609/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to symmetric theory, we decompose the problem into 136 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 609. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
